func (e *eval) evalNot(iter evalIterator) error {
	expr := e.query[e.index]

	if e.unknown(expr, e.bindings) {
		return e.evalNotPartial(iter)
	}

	negation := ast.NewBody(expr.ComplementNoWith())
	child := evalPool.Get()
	defer evalPool.Put(child)

	e.closure(negation, child)

	if e.traceEnabled {
		child.traceEnter(negation)
	}

	if err := child.eval(func(*eval) error {
		if e.traceEnabled {
			child.traceExit(negation)
			child.traceRedo(negation)
		}
		child.defined = true

		return nil
	}); err != nil {
		return err
	}

	if !child.defined {
		return iter(e)
	}

	child.defined = false

	e.traceFail(expr)
	return nil
}

func (e *eval) evalWith(iter evalIterator) error {

	expr := e.query[e.index]

	var disable []ast.Ref

	if e.partial() {
		// Avoid the `disable` var to escape to heap unless partial evaluation is enabled.
		var disablePartial []ast.Ref
		// Disable inlining on all references in the expression so the result of
		// partial evaluation has the same semantics w/ the with statements
		// preserved.
		disableRef := func(x ast.Ref) bool {
			disablePartial = append(disablePartial, x.GroundPrefix())
			return false
		}

		// If the value is unknown the with statement cannot be evaluated and so
		// the entire expression should be saved to be safe. In the future this
		// could be relaxed in certain cases (e.g., if the with statement would
		// have no effect.)
		for _, with := range expr.With {
			if isFunction(e.compiler.TypeEnv, with.Target) || // non-builtin function replaced
				isOtherRef(with.Target) { // built-in replaced

				ast.WalkRefs(with.Value, disableRef)
				continue
			}

			// with target is data or input (not built-in)
			if e.saveSet.ContainsRecursive(with.Value, e.bindings) {
				return e.saveExprMarkUnknowns(expr, e.bindings, func() error {
					return e.next(iter)
				})
			}
			ast.WalkRefs(with.Target, disableRef)
			ast.WalkRefs(with.Value, disableRef)
		}

		ast.WalkRefs(expr.NoWith(), disableRef)

		disable = disablePartial
	}

	pairsInput := [][2]*ast.Term{}
	pairsData := [][2]*ast.Term{}
	targets := make([]ast.Ref, 0, len(expr.With))

	var functionMocks [][2]*ast.Term

	for i := range expr.With {
		target := expr.With[i].Target
		plugged := e.bindings.Plug(expr.With[i].Value)
		switch {
		// NOTE(sr): ordering matters here: isFunction's ref is also covered by isDataRef
		case isFunction(e.compiler.TypeEnv, target):
			functionMocks = append(functionMocks, [...]*ast.Term{target, plugged})

		case isInputRef(target):
			pairsInput = append(pairsInput, [...]*ast.Term{target, plugged})

		case isDataRef(target):
			pairsData = append(pairsData, [...]*ast.Term{target, plugged})

		default: // target must be builtin
			if _, _, ok := e.builtinFunc(target.String()); ok {
				functionMocks = append(functionMocks, [...]*ast.Term{target, plugged})
				continue // don't append to disabled targets below
			}
		}
		targets = append(targets, target.Value.(ast.Ref))
	}

	input, err := mergeTermWithValues(e.input, pairsInput)
	if err != nil {
		return &Error{
			Code:     ConflictErr,
			Location: expr.Location,
			Message:  err.Error(),
		}
	}

	data, err := mergeTermWithValues(e.data, pairsData)
	if err != nil {
		return &Error{
			Code:     ConflictErr,
			Location: expr.Location,
			Message:  err.Error(),

225:func (e *eval) closure(query ast.Body, cpy *eval) {
	fmt.Fprintf(s, "<query: %v index: %d findOne: %v", e.query, e.index, e.findOne)
	if e.parent != nil {
		s.WriteByte(' ')
		e.parent.string(s)
	}
	s.WriteByte('>')

func (e *eval) builtinFunc(name string) (*ast.Builtin, BuiltinFunc, bool) {
	decl, ok := ast.BuiltinMap[name]
	if ok {
		if f, ok := builtinFunctions[name]; ok {
			return decl, f, true
		}
		if bi, ok := e.builtins[name]; ok {
			return decl, bi.Func, true
		}
	}
	if bi, ok := e.builtins[name]; ok {
		return bi.Decl, bi.Func, true
	}

	return nil, nil, false
}

func (e *eval) closure(query ast.Body, cpy *eval) {
	*cpy = *e
	cpy.index = 0
	cpy.query = query
	cpy.queryID = cpy.queryIDFact.Next()
	cpy.parent = e
	cpy.findOne = false
}

// This reduces memory waste when evaluating functions or rules with known argument counts.

//go:build profile

package topdown

import (
	"context"
	"testing"

	"github.com/open-policy-agent/opa/v1/ast"
)

type scratchProfiler struct {
	enters   []string
	succeeds []string
	fails    []string
}

func (s *scratchProfiler) EnterRule(r *ast.Rule)   { s.enters = append(s.enters, r.Path().String()) }
func (s *scratchProfiler) SucceedRule(r *ast.Rule) { s.succeeds = append(s.succeeds, r.Path().String()) }
func (s *scratchProfiler) FailRule(r *ast.Rule)    { s.fails = append(s.fails, r.Path().String()) }

func TestScratchTopdownProfile(t *testing.T) {
	module := `package authz
deny if { input.user == "banned" }
allow if { input.user == "admin" }
`
	compiler := ast.NewCompiler()
	if compiler.Compile(map[string]*ast.Module{
		"test.rego": ast.MustParseModule(module),
	}); compiler.Failed() {
		t.Fatal(compiler.Errors)
	}

	queries := []string{
		"data.authz.deny",
		"not data.authz.deny",
		"data.authz.allow",
		"data.authz",
	}
	for _, qs := range queries {
		body := ast.MustParseBody(qs)
		p := &scratchProfiler{}
		q := NewQuery(body).
			WithCompiler(compiler).
			WithInput(ast.NewTerm(ast.NewObject(
				[2]*ast.Term{ast.StringTerm("user"), ast.StringTerm("admin")},
			))).
			WithRuleProfiler(p)
		rs, err := q.Run(context.Background())
		t.Logf("query=%q results=%d err=%v", qs, len(rs), err)
		t.Logf("  enters=%v", p.enters)
		t.Logf("  succeeds=%v", p.succeeds)
		t.Logf("  fails=%v", p.fails)
	}
}
EOF

//go:build profile

package topdown

import (
	"context"
	"testing"

	"github.com/open-policy-agent/opa/v1/ast"
	"github.com/open-policy-agent/opa/v1/storage/inmem"
)

func TestScratchTopdownTrace(t *testing.T) {
	module := `package authz
deny if { input.user == "banned" }
allow if { input.user == "admin" }
`
	compiler := ast.NewCompiler()
	if compiler.Compile(map[string]*ast.Module{
		"test.rego": ast.MustParseModule(module),
	}); compiler.Failed() {
		t.Fatal(compiler.Errors)
	}

	body := ast.MustParseBody("data.authz.deny")
	tr := NewBufferTracer()
	q := NewQuery(body).
		WithCompiler(compiler).
		WithStore(inmem.New()).
		WithQueryTracer(tr).
		WithInput(ast.NewTerm(ast.NewObject(
			[2]*ast.Term{ast.StringTerm("user"), ast.StringTerm("admin")},
		)))
	rs, err := q.Run(context.Background())
	t.Logf("results=%d err=%v", len(rs), err)
	for _, ev := range *tr {
		t.Logf("%v %v %v", ev.Op, ev.Node, ev.Message)
	}
}
EOF

//go:build profile

package rego

import (
	"context"
	"testing"
)

func TestScratchProfile(t *testing.T) {
	ctx := context.Background()
	module := `package authz

allow if {
	input.user == "admin"
}

deny if {
	false
}

multi if {
	false
}

multi if {
	true
}

roles contains x if {
	x := input.roles[_]
}
`
	r := New(
		Module("test.rego", module),
		Input(map[string]any{"user": "admin", "roles": []any{"a", "b", "c"}}),
		EnableRuleProfile(true),
		Query("data.authz"),
	)
	rs, err := r.Eval(ctx)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("eval: %v", err)
	}
	if len(rs) == 0 {
		t.Fatal("no results")
	}
	p := rs[0].Profile
	if p == nil {
		t.Fatal("profile is nil")
	}
	t.Logf("summary: %s", p.Summary())
	t.Logf("paths: %v", p.RulePaths())
	for _, path := range p.RulePaths() {
		t.Logf("  %s: %s", path, p.Stat(path))
	}
	if !p.ContainsRule("data.authz.deny") {
		t.Errorf("deny missing (failed rule should appear)")
	}
	deny := p.Stat("data.authz.deny")
	if deny == nil || deny.Evals != 1 || deny.Successes != 0 {
		t.Errorf("deny stat = %v", deny)
	}
	roles := p.Stat("data.authz.roles")
	if roles == nil || roles.Evals != 1 || roles.Successes != 3 {
		t.Errorf("roles stat = %v", roles)
	}
	multi := p.Stat("data.authz.multi")
	if multi == nil || multi.Evals != 2 || multi.Successes != 1 {
		t.Errorf("multi stat = %v", multi)
	}
	t.Logf("packages: %v", p.Packages())
	t.Logf("failed: %v", p.FailedRules())
	t.Logf("succeeded: %v", p.SucceededRules())
	t.Logf("hot(1): %v", p.HotRules(1))
	t.Logf("overall: %v", p.OverallSuccessRate())
	t.Logf("string:\n%s", p.String())
	t.Logf("packagestats: %v", p.PackageStats())
}

func TestScratchProfilePreparedEval(t *testing.T) {
	ctx := context.Background()
	module := `package authz
allow if { input.user == "admin" }
`
	r := New(
		Module("test.rego", module),
		Query("data.authz.allow"),
	)
	pq, err := r.PrepareForEval(ctx)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("prepare: %v", err)
	}
	rs, err := pq.Eval(ctx, EvalInput(map[string]any{"user": "admin"}), EvalRuleProfile(true))
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("eval: %v", err)
	}
	if len(rs) == 0 {
		t.Fatal("no results")
	}
	if rs[0].Profile == nil {
		t.Fatal("profile nil")
	}
	t.Logf("summary: %s", rs[0].Profile.Summary())
	if rs[0].Profile.Stat("data.authz.allow") == nil {
		t.Fatalf("allow missing")
	}
}

		Query("data.authz.allow"),
	)
	rs, err := r.Eval(ctx)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("eval: %v", err)
	}
	if len(rs) > 0 && rs[0].Profile != nil {
		t.Fatalf("profile should be nil when disabled, got %v", rs[0].Profile)
	}
}

COMMAND:
cd /app && cat > v1/rego/profile_methods_scratch_test.go <<'EOF'
//go:build profile

package rego

import (
	"reflect"
	"testing"
)

func TestScratchProfileMethods(t *testing.T) {
	p := NewEvalProfile()
	p.increment("data.authz.allow", 5, 3)
	p.increment("data.authz.allow", 2, 1)
	p.increment("data.authz.deny", 4, 0)
	p.increment("data.other.thing", 1, 1)

	if got := p.Stat("data.authz.allow").Evals; got != 7 {
		t.Errorf("allow evals = %d", got)
	}
	if got := p.Stat("data.authz.allow").Successes; got != 4 {
		t.Errorf("allow successes = %d", got)
	}
	if p.Stat("nope") != nil {
		t.Errorf("Stat nope should be nil")
	}

	paths := p.RulePaths()
	want := []string{"data.authz.allow", "data.authz.deny", "data.other.thing"}
	if !reflect.DeepEqual(paths, want) {
		t.Errorf("RulePaths = %v, want %v", paths, want)
	}

	if got := p.SuccessRate("data.authz.allow"); got != 4.0/7.0 {
		t.Errorf("SuccessRate = %v", got)
	}
	if p.SuccessRate("nope") != 0 {
		t.Errorf("SuccessRate nope != 0")
	}
	if got := p.OverallSuccessRate(); got != 5.0/12.0 {
		t.Errorf("OverallSuccessRate = %v", got)
	}

	hot := p.HotRules(4)
	want = []string{"data.authz.allow", "data.authz.deny"}
	if !reflect.DeepEqual(hot, want) {
		t.Errorf("HotRules(4) = %v, want %v", hot, want)
	}
	if p.HotRules(100) != nil {
		t.Errorf("HotRules(100) should be nil")
	}
	failed := p.FailedRules()
	want = []string{"data.authz.deny"}
	if !reflect.DeepEqual(failed, want) {
		t.Errorf("FailedRules = %v, want %v", failed, want)
	}
	succeeded := p.SucceededRules()
	want = []string{"data.authz.allow", "data.other.thing"}
	if !reflect.DeepEqual(succeeded, want) {
		t.Errorf("SucceededRules = %v, want %v", succeeded, want)
	}

	pkgs := p.Packages()
	want = []string{"data.authz", "data.other"}
	if !reflect.DeepEqual(pkgs, want) {
		t.Errorf("Packages = %v, want %v", pkgs, want)
	}

	filtered := p.FilterByPackage("data.authz")
	if filtered == nil {
		t.Fatal("filtered nil")
	}
	if len(filtered.RulePaths()) != 2 {
		t.Errorf("filtered paths = %v", filtered.RulePaths())
	}
	// deep copy: modify original, filtered should not change
	p.increment("data.authz.allow", 1, 0)
	if got := filtered.Stat("data.authz.allow").Evals; got != 7 {
		t.Errorf("filtered not deep copied: evals = %d", got)
	}

	merged := p.Merge(NewEvalProfile())
	if merged.Stat("data.authz.allow").Evals != 8 {
		t.Errorf("merge evals = %d", merged.Stat("data.authz.allow").Evals)
	}

	if !p.ContainsRule("data.authz.deny") {
		t.Errorf("ContainsRule deny false")
	}
	if p.ContainsRule("nope") {
		t.Errorf("ContainsRule nope true")
	}

	if got := p.Summary(); got != "profile: 3 rules, 13 evals, 5 successes" {
		t.Errorf("Summary = %q", got)
	}

	gotStr := p.String()
	wantStr := "Profile:\n  data.authz.allow: evals=8 successes=4\n  data.authz.deny: evals=4 successes=0\n  data.other.thing: evals=1 successes=1\n"
	if gotStr != wantStr {
		t.Errorf("String = %q, want %q", gotStr, wantStr)
	}

	if !p.Equal(p) {
		t.Errorf("Equal self false")
	}
	q := NewEvalProfile()
	q.increment("data.authz.allow", 8, 4)
	q.increment("data.authz.deny", 4, 0)
	q.increment("data.other.thing", 1, 1)
	if !p.Equal(q) {
		t.Errorf("Equal structurally same false")
	}
	q.increment("data.authz.deny", 1, 0)
	if p.Equal(q) {
		t.Errorf("Equal structurally different true")
	}

	ps := p.PackageStats()
	if ps["data.authz"].Evals != 12 || ps["data.authz"].Successes != 4 {
		t.Errorf("PackageStats = %v", ps)
	}
	if ps["data.other"].Evals != 1 || ps["data.other"].Successes != 1 {
		t.Errorf("PackageStats other = %v", ps)
	}
}

func TestScratchNilMethods(t *testing.T) {
	var p *EvalProfile
	var s *RuleStat
	var d *ProfileDiff
	if p.Stat("x") != nil {
		t.Error("nil Stat")
	}
	if p.RulePaths() != nil {
		t.Error("nil RulePaths")
	}
	if p.SuccessRate("x") != 0 {
		t.Error("nil SuccessRate")
	}
	if p.OverallSuccessRate() != 0 {
		t.Error("nil OverallSuccessRate")
	}
	if p.HotRules(1) != nil {
		t.Error("nil HotRules")
	}
	if p.FailedRules() != nil {
		t.Error("nil FailedRules")
	}
	if p.SucceededRules() != nil {
		t.Error("nil SucceededRules")
	}
	if p.Packages() != nil {
		t.Error("nil Packages")
	}
	if p.FilterByPackage("x") != nil {
		t.Error("nil FilterByPackage")
	}
	if p.PackageStats() != nil {
		t.Error("nil PackageStats")
	}
	if p.ContainsRule("x") {
		t.Error("nil ContainsRule true")
	}
	if p.Summary() != "profile: disabled" {
		t.Errorf("nil Summary = %q", p.Summary())
	}
	if p.String() != "<nil>" {
		t.Errorf("nil String = %q", p.String())
	}
	if p.Diff(NewEvalProfile()) != nil {
		t.Error("nil Diff should return nil")
	}
	if p.Equal(nil) != true {
		t.Error("nil Equal nil should be true")
	}
	if p.Equal(NewEvalProfile()) != false {
		t.Error("nil Equal non-nil should be false")
	}
	if s.SuccessRate() != 0 {
		t.Error("nil RuleStat SuccessRate")
	}
	if s.String() != "<nil>" {
		t.Errorf("nil RuleStat String = %q", s.String())
	}
	if d.HasChanges() {
		t.Error("nil ProfileDiff HasChanges true")
	}
}

func TestScratchDiff(t *testing.T) {
	a := NewEvalProfile()
	a.increment("data.a", 2, 1)
	a.increment("data.b", 3, 2)
	a.increment("data.c", 5, 5)

	b := NewEvalProfile()
	b.increment("data.b", 3, 2) // same
	b.increment("data.c", 6, 5) // changed
	b.increment("data.d", 1, 1) // added

	diff := a.Diff(b)
	if diff == nil {
		t.Fatal("diff nil")
	}
	if !diff.HasChanges() {
		t.Error("HasChanges false")
	}
	if len(diff.Added) != 1 || diff.Added["data.d"] == nil {
		t.Errorf("Added = %v", diff.Added)
	}
	if len(diff.Removed) != 1 || diff.Removed["data.a"] == nil {
		t.Errorf("Removed = %v", diff.Removed)
	}
	if len(diff.Changed) != 1 || diff.Changed["data.c"] == nil {
		t.Errorf("Changed = %v", diff.Changed)
	}
	if diff.Changed["data.c"].EvalsDelta != 1 || diff.Changed["data.c"].SuccessesDelta != 0 {
		t.Errorf("Changed delta = %+v", diff.Changed["data.c"])
	}

	// no changes
	diff2 := a.Diff(a)
	if diff2 == nil || diff2.HasChanges() {
		t.Errorf("no-change diff = %+v", diff2)
	}
	if diff2.Added != nil || diff2.Removed != nil || diff2.Changed != nil {
		t.Errorf("no-change fields not nil: %+v", diff2)
	}
}

func TestScratchMerge(t *testing.T) {
	var nilp *EvalProfile
	if nilp.Merge(nil) != nil {
		t.Error("both nil merge should be nil")
	}
	p := NewEvalProfile()
	p.increment("data.a", 1, 0)
	if nilp.Merge(p) != p {
		t.Error("nil merge should return non-nil side")
	}
	if p.Merge(nil) != p {
		t.Error("merge nil should return receiver")
	}
	q := NewEvalProfile()
	q.increment("data.a", 2, 2)
	q.increment("data.b", 1, 1)
	m := p.Merge(q)
	if m.Stat("data.a").Evals != 3 || m.Stat("data.a").Successes != 2 {
		t.Errorf("merged a = %v", m.Stat("data.a"))
	}
	if m.Stat("data.b") == nil {
		t.Error("merged b missing")
	}
}
EOF

//go:build profile

package rego

import (
	"context"
	"testing"
)

func TestScratchRepeatedEval(t *testing.T) {
	ctx := context.Background()
	module := `package authz
allow if { true }
`
	r := New(
		Module("test.rego", module),
		EnableRuleProfile(true),
		Query("data.authz.allow"),
	)
	rs, err := r.Eval(ctx)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	p := rs[0].Profile
	stat := p.Stat("data.authz.allow")
	if stat.Evals != 1 || stat.Successes != 1 {
		t.Errorf("expected 1/1 got %v", stat)
	}
	// Evaluate again on a fresh Rego? Same Rego Eval twice should create a fresh profile each time.
	rs2, err := r.Eval(ctx)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	stat2 := rs2[0].Profile.Stat("data.authz.allow")
	if stat2.Evals != 1 || stat2.Successes != 1 {
		t.Errorf("second eval expected 1/1 got %v", stat2)
	}
}

func TestScratchPreparedConstruction(t *testing.T) {
	ctx := context.Background()
	module := `package authz
allow if { true }
`
	r := New(
		Module("test.rego", module),
		EnableRuleProfile(true),
		Query("data.authz.allow"),
	)
	pq, err := r.PrepareForEval(ctx)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	rs, err := pq.Eval(ctx)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if rs[0].Profile == nil {
		t.Fatal("profile nil")
	}
	t.Logf("summary: %s", rs[0].Profile.Summary())
}
EOF

		return nil
	}"""

	}

	}"""

//go:build profile

package rego

import (
	"context"
	"testing"
)

func TestScratchOverride(t *testing.T) {
	ctx := context.Background()
	module := `package authz
allow if { true }
`
	r := New(
		Module("test.rego", module),
		EnableRuleProfile(true),
		Query("data.authz.allow"),
	)
	pq, err := r.PrepareForEval(ctx)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	// enabled by construction
	rs, err := pq.Eval(ctx)
	if err != nil || len(rs) == 0 || rs[0].Profile == nil {
		t.Fatalf("expected profile enabled, rs=%v err=%v", len(rs), err)
	}
	// disabled per-eval
	rs2, err := pq.Eval(ctx, EvalRuleProfile(false))
	if err != nil || len(rs2) == 0 || rs2[0].Profile != nil {
		t.Fatalf("expected profile disabled, rs=%v err=%v", len(rs2), err)
	}
	// re-enabled per-eval
	rs3, err := pq.Eval(ctx, EvalRuleProfile(true))
	if err != nil || len(rs3) == 0 || rs3[0].Profile == nil {
		t.Fatalf("expected profile re-enabled, rs=%v err=%v", len(rs3), err)
	}
}
EOF
timeout 28 go test -tags profile ./v1/rego/ -run 'TestScratchOverride' -v 2>&1 | tail -20
rm -f v1/rego/profile_override_scratch_test.go
OUTPUT:

--- PASS: TestScratchOverride (0.00s)
PASS

	// record the profiler results for the previous expression
	p.calculateHitsByExprIndex()

	file := p.prevExpr.location.File
	hits, ok := p.hits[file]
	if !ok {
		hits = map[int]ExprStats{}
		hits[p.prevExpr.location.Row] = getProfilerStats(p.prevExpr, p.activeTimer)
		p.hits[file] = hits
	} else {
		pos := p.prevExpr.location.Row
		pStats, ok := hits[pos]
		if !ok {
			hits[pos] = getProfilerStats(p.prevExpr, p.activeTimer)
		} else {
			pStats.ExprTimeNs += time.Since(p.activeTimer).Nanoseconds()

			switch p.prevExpr.op {
			case topdown.EvalOp:
				pStats.NumEval++
			case topdown.RedoOp:
				pStats.NumRedo++
			}
			hits[pos] = pStats
		}
	}

	// reset active timer and expression
	p.activeTimer = time.Now()
	p.prevExpr = exprInfo{
		op:       eventType,
		location: expr.Location,
		index:    expr.Index,
	}
}

func (p *Profiler) processLastExpr() {
	expr := ast.Expr{
		Location: p.prevExpr.location,
		Index:    p.prevExpr.index,
	}
	p.processExpr(&expr, p.prevExpr.op)
}

func (p *Profiler) calculateHitsByExprIndex() {
	file := p.prevExpr.location.File
	hitsUnique, ok := p.hitsByExprIndex[file]

	if !ok {
		hitsUnique = map[int]map[int]ExprStats{}
		hitsUnique[p.prevExpr.location.Row] = map[int]ExprStats{p.prevExpr.index: getProfilerStats(p.prevExpr, p.activeTimer)}
		p.hitsByExprIndex[file] = hitsUnique
	} else {
		row := p.prevExpr.location.Row
		idx := p.prevExpr.index

		pStats, ok := hitsUnique[row]
		if !ok {
			hitsUnique[row] = map[int]ExprStats{idx: getProfilerStats(p.prevExpr, p.activeTimer)}
		} else {
			pStatsIdx, ok := pStats[idx]
			if !ok {
				hitsUnique[row][idx] = getProfilerStats(p.prevExpr, p.activeTimer)
			} else {
				pStatsIdx.ExprTimeNs += time.Since(p.activeTimer).Nanoseconds()

				switch p.prevExpr.op {
				case topdown.EvalOp:
					pStatsIdx.NumEval++
				case topdown.RedoOp:
					pStatsIdx.NumRedo++
				}

				hitsUnique[row][idx] = pStatsIdx
			}
		}
	}
}

func getProfilerStats(expr exprInfo, timer time.Time) ExprStats {
	profilerStats := ExprStats{}
	profilerStats.ExprTimeNs = time.Since(timer).Nanoseconds()
	profilerStats.Location = expr.location

	switch expr.op {
	case topdown.EvalOp:
		profilerStats.NumEval = 1
	case topdown.RedoOp:
		profilerStats.NumRedo = 1
	}
	return profilerStats
}

// ExprStats represents the result of profiling an expression.
type ExprStats struct {
	ExprTimeNs int64         `json:"total_time_ns"`
	NumEval    int           `json:"num_eval"`
	NumRedo    int           `json:"num_redo"`
	NumGenExpr int           `json:"num_gen_expr"`
	Location   *ast.Location `json:"location"`
}

cd /app && python3 - <<'PYEOF'
path = 'v1/rego/profile.go'
with open(path) as f:
    s = f.read()

old = """// ruleProfilerAdapter adapts an EvalProfile to the topdown.RuleProfiler
// interface.
type ruleProfilerAdapter struct {
	profile *EvalProfile
}

func (a *ruleProfilerAdapter) EnterRule(rule *ast.Rule) {
	if a == nil || a.profile == nil || rule == nil {
		return
	}
	a.profile.increment(rule.Path().String(), 1, 0)
}

func (a *ruleProfilerAdapter) SucceedRule(rule *ast.Rule) {
	if a == nil || a.profile == nil || rule == nil {
		return
	}
	a.profile.increment(rule.Path().String(), 0, 1)
}

func (a *ruleProfilerAdapter) FailRule(*ast.Rule) {}
"""
new = """// ruleProfilerAdapter adapts an EvalProfile to the topdown.RuleProfiler
// interface.
//
// Successes are counted once per rule entry that produces at least one result
// (rather than once per result). The stack tracks rule entries so that
// recursive rules and rules producing multiple results are counted correctly.
type ruleProfilerAdapter struct {
	profile *EvalProfile
	stack   []*ast.Rule
}

func (a *ruleProfilerAdapter) EnterRule(rule *ast.Rule) {
	if a == nil || a.profile == nil || rule == nil {
		return
	}
	a.profile.increment(rule.Path().String(), 1, 0)
	a.stack = append(a.stack, rule)
}

func (a *ruleProfilerAdapter) SucceedRule(rule *ast.Rule) {
	if a == nil || a.profile == nil || rule == nil {
		return
	}
	if n := len(a.stack); n > 0 && a.stack[n-1] == rule {
		a.stack = a.stack[:n-1]
		a.profile.increment(rule.Path().String(), 0, 1)
	}
}

func (a *ruleProfilerAdapter) FailRule(rule *ast.Rule) {
	if a == nil || a.profile == nil || rule == nil {
		return
	}
	if n := len(a.stack); n > 0 && a.stack[n-1] == rule {
		a.stack = a.stack[:n-1]
	}
}
"""
assert old in s
s = s.replace(old, new)
with open(path, 'w') as f:
    f.write(s)
print("done")
PYEOF
timeout 25 go build -tags profile ./v1/rego/ ./rego/ ./v1/topdown/
OUTPUT:
done

//go:build profile

package rego

import (
	"context"
	"testing"
)

func TestScratchSemantics(t *testing.T) {
	ctx := context.Background()
	module := `package authz
allow if { true }
deny if { false }
multi if { false }
multi if { true }
roles contains x if { x := input.roles[_] }
`
	r := New(
		Module("test.rego", module),
		Input(map[string]any{"roles": []any{"a", "b", "c"}}),
		EnableRuleProfile(true),
		Query("data.authz"),
	)
	rs, err := r.Eval(ctx)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if len(rs) == 0 {
		t.Fatal("no results")
	}
	p := rs[0].Profile
	t.Logf("summary: %s", p.Summary())
	for _, path := range p.RulePaths() {
		t.Logf("  %s: %s", path, p.Stat(path))
	}
	if got := p.Stat("data.authz.roles").Successes; got != 1 {
		t.Errorf("roles successes = %d, want 1", got)
	}
	if got := p.Stat("data.authz.multi").Successes; got != 1 {
		t.Errorf("multi successes = %d, want 1", got)
	}
	if got := p.Stat("data.authz.multi").Evals; got != 2 {
		t.Errorf("multi evals = %d, want 2", got)
	}
	if got := p.Stat("data.authz.deny").Successes; got != 0 {
		t.Errorf("deny successes = %d, want 0", got)
	}
	if got := p.Stat("data.authz.deny").Evals; got != 1 {
		t.Errorf("deny evals = %d, want 1", got)
	}
}

//go:build profile

package topdown

import (
	"context"
	"testing"

	"github.com/open-policy-agent/opa/v1/ast"
	"github.com/open-policy-agent/opa/v1/storage/inmem"
)

type sp struct{ enters []string }

func (s *sp) EnterRule(r *ast.Rule)   { s.enters = append(s.enters, r.Path().String()) }
func (s *sp) SucceedRule(r *ast.Rule) {}
func (s *sp) FailRule(r *ast.Rule)    {}

func TestScratchNotWithTrace(t *testing.T) {
	module := `package authz
allow if { input.user == "admin" }
`
	compiler := ast.NewCompiler()
	if compiler.Compile(map[string]*ast.Module{"test.rego": ast.MustParseModule(module)}); compiler.Failed() {
		t.Fatal(compiler.Errors)
	}
	body := ast.MustParseBody(`data.authz.allow with input as {"user": "admin"}; not data.authz.allow with input as {"user": "banned"}`)
	tr := NewBufferTracer()
	p := &sp{}
	q := NewQuery(body).
		WithCompiler(compiler).
		WithStore(inmem.New()).
		WithRuleProfiler(p).
		WithQueryTracer(tr).
		WithInput(ast.NewTerm(ast.NewObject([2]*ast.Term{ast.StringTerm("user"), ast.StringTerm("admin")})))
	rs, err := q.Run(context.Background())
	t.Logf("results=%d err=%v enters=%v", len(rs), err, p.enters)
	for _, ev := range *tr {
		t.Logf("%v %v %v", ev.Op, ev.Node, ev.Message)
	}
}
EOF

// Copyright 2024 The OPA Authors.  All rights reserved.
// Use of this source code is governed by an Apache2
// license that can be found in the LICENSE file.

//go:build profile

package rego

import (
	"context"
	"reflect"
	"testing"
)

func TestProfileEvaluation(t *testing.T) {
	ctx := context.Background()
	module := `package authz

allow if {
	input.user == "admin"
}

deny if {
	false
}

multi if {
	false
}

multi if {
	true
}

roles contains x if {
	x := input.roles[_]
}
`
	r := New(
		Module("test.rego", module),
		Input(map[string]any{"user": "admin", "roles": []any{"a", "b", "c"}}),
		EnableRuleProfile(true),
		Query("data.authz"),
	)

	rs, err := r.Eval(ctx)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("eval: %v", err)
	}
	if len(rs) == 0 {
		t.Fatal("expected results")
	}

	profile := rs[0].Profile
	if profile == nil {
		t.Fatal("expected profile to be set")
	}

	expected := map[string]RuleStat{
		"data.authz.allow": {Evals: 1, Successes: 1},
		"data.authz.deny":  {Evals: 1, Successes: 0},
		"data.authz.multi": {Evals: 2, Successes: 1},
		"data.authz.roles": {Evals: 1, Successes: 1},
	}

	for path, want := range expected {
		stat := profile.Stat(path)
		if stat == nil {
			t.Errorf("missing stat for %s", path)
			continue
		}
		if *stat != want {
			t.Errorf("stat for %s = %v, want %v", path, *stat, want)
		}
	}

	if got := profile.Summary(); got != "profile: 4 rules, 5 evals, 3 successes" {
		t.Errorf("summary = %q", got)
	}

	if got := profile.FailedRules(); !reflect.DeepEqual(got, []string{"data.authz.deny"}) {
		t.Errorf("failed rules = %v", got)
	}

	if got := profile.SucceededRules(); !reflect.DeepEqual(got, []string{"data.authz.allow", "data.authz.multi", "data.authz.roles"}) {
		t.Errorf("succeeded rules = %v", got)
	}

	if got := profile.Packages(); !reflect.DeepEqual(got, []string{"data.authz"}) {
		t.Errorf("packages = %v", got)
	}
}

func TestProfileDisabled(t *testing.T) {
	ctx := context.Background()
	r := New(
		Module("test.rego", `package authz
allow if { true }`),
		Query("data.authz.allow"),
	)

	rs, err := r.Eval(ctx)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("eval: %v", err)
	}
	if len(rs) == 0 {
		t.Fatal("expected results")
	}
	if rs[0].Profile != nil {
		t.Fatalf("expected nil profile, got %v", rs[0].Profile)
	}
}

func TestProfilePreparedEval(t *testing.T) {
	ctx := context.Background()
	r := New(
		Module("test.rego", `package authz
allow if { input.user == "admin" }`),
		Query("data.authz.allow"),
	)

	pq, err := r.PrepareForEval(ctx)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("prepare: %v", err)
	}

	rs, err := pq.Eval(ctx, EvalInput(map[string]any{"user": "admin"}), EvalRuleProfile(true))
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("eval: %v", err)
	}
	if len(rs) == 0 {
		t.Fatal("expected results")
	}
	if rs[0].Profile == nil {
		t.Fatal("expected profile to be set")
	}
	if stat := rs[0].Profile.Stat("data.authz.allow"); stat == nil || *stat != (RuleStat{Evals: 1, Successes: 1}) {
		t.Errorf("unexpected allow stat: %v", stat)
	}

	// Per-eval option should override the construction option.
	r2 := New(
		Module("test.rego", `package authz
allow if { true }`),
		EnableRuleProfile(true),
		Query("data.authz.allow"),
	)
	pq2, err := r2.PrepareForEval(ctx)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("prepare: %v", err)
	}
	rs2, err := pq2.Eval(ctx, EvalRuleProfile(false))
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("eval: %v", err)
	}
	if len(rs2) == 0 {
		t.Fatal("expected results")
	}
	if rs2[0].Profile != nil {
		t.Fatalf("expected nil profile when disabled per-eval, got %v", rs2[0].Profile)
	}
}

func TestProfileMethods(t *testing.T) {
	p := NewEvalProfile()
	p.increment("data.authz.allow", 7, 4)
	p.increment("data.authz.deny", 4, 0)
	p.increment("data.other.thing", 1, 1)

	if got := p.Stat("data.authz.allow").Evals; got != 7 {
		t.Errorf("allow evals = %d", got)
	}
	if p.Stat("nope") != nil {
		t.Errorf("expected nil for untracked rule")
	}
	if got := p.RulePaths(); !reflect.DeepEqual(got, []string{"data.authz.allow", "data.authz.deny", "data.other.thing"}) {
		t.Errorf("rule paths = %v", got)
	}
	if got := p.SuccessRate("data.authz.allow"); got != 4.0/7.0 {
		t.Errorf("success rate = %v", got)
	}
	if got := p.OverallSuccessRate(); got != 5.0/12.0 {
		t.Errorf("overall success rate = %v", got)
	}
	if got := p.HotRules(4); !reflect.DeepEqual(got, []string{"data.authz.allow", "data.authz.deny"}) {
		t.Errorf("hot rules = %v", got)
	}
	if p.HotRules(100) != nil {
		t.Errorf("expected nil hot rules")
	}
	if got := p.FailedRules(); !reflect.DeepEqual(got, []string{"data.authz.deny"}) {
		t.Errorf("failed rules = %v", got)
	}
	if got := p.SucceededRules(); !reflect.DeepEqual(got, []string{"data.authz.allow", "data.other.thing"}) {
		t.Errorf("succeeded rules = %v", got)
	}
	if got := p.Packages(); !reflect.DeepEqual(got, []string{"data.authz", "data.other"}) {
		t.Errorf("packages = %v", got)
	}

	filtered := p.FilterByPackage("data.authz")
	if filtered == nil || len(filtered.RulePaths()) != 2 {
		t.Fatalf("filtered = %v", filtered)
	}
	p.increment("data.authz.allow", 1, 0)
	if got := filtered.Stat("data.authz.allow").Evals; got != 7 {
		t.Errorf("filtered profile not deep-copied: evals = %d", got)
	}

	if !p.ContainsRule("data.authz.deny") || p.ContainsRule("nope") {
		t.Errorf("contains rule returned unexpected result")
	}

	if got := p.Summary(); got != "profile: 3 rules, 13 evals, 5 successes" {
		t.Errorf("summary = %q", got)
	}

	want := "Profile:\n  data.authz.allow: evals=8 successes=4\n  data.authz.deny: evals=4 successes=0\n  data.other.thing: evals=1 successes=1\n"
	if got := p.String(); got != want {
		t.Errorf("string = %q, want %q", got, want)
	}

	other := NewEvalProfile()
	other.increment("data.authz.allow", 8, 4)
	other.increment("data.authz.deny", 4, 0)
	other.increment("data.other.thing", 1, 1)
	if !p.Equal(other) {
		t.Errorf("expected structurally equal profiles")
	}
	other.increment("data.authz.deny", 1, 0)
	if p.Equal(other) {
		t.Errorf("expected structurally different profiles")
	}

	stats := p.PackageStats()
	if stats["data.authz"].Evals != 12 || stats["data.authz"].Successes != 4 {
		t.Errorf("package stats = %v", stats)
	}
	if stats["data.other"].Evals != 1 || stats["data.other"].Successes != 1 {
		t.Errorf("package stats other = %v", stats)
	}
}

func TestProfileNilReceiver(t *testing.T) {
	var p *EvalProfile
	var s *RuleStat
	var d *ProfileDiff

	if p.Stat("x") != nil {
		t.Errorf("nil Stat")
	}
	if p.RulePaths() != nil {
		t.Errorf("nil RulePaths")
	}
	if p.SuccessRate("x") != 0 {
		t.Errorf("nil SuccessRate")
	}
	if p.OverallSuccessRate() != 0 {
		t.Errorf("nil OverallSuccessRate")
	}
	if p.HotRules(1) != nil || p.FailedRules() != nil || p.SucceededRules() != nil || p.Packages() != nil {
		t.Errorf("nil slice-returning method returned non-nil")
	}
	if p.FilterByPackage("x") != nil || p.PackageStats() != nil {
		t.Errorf("nil map-returning method returned non-nil")
	}
	if p.ContainsRule("x") {
		t.Errorf("nil ContainsRule")
	}
	if p.Summary() != "profile: disabled" {
		t.Errorf("nil Summary = %q", p.Summary())
	}
	if p.String() != "<nil>" {
		t.Errorf("nil String = %q", p.String())
	}
	if p.Diff(NewEvalProfile()) != nil {
		t.Errorf("nil Diff should be nil")
	}
	if !p.Equal(nil) {
		t.Errorf("nil Equal nil should be true")
	}
	if p.Equal(NewEvalProfile()) {
		t.Errorf("nil Equal non-nil should be false")
	}
	if s.SuccessRate() != 0 {
		t.Errorf("nil RuleStat SuccessRate")
	}
	if s.String() != "<nil>" {
		t.Errorf("nil RuleStat String = %q", s.String())
	}
	if d.HasChanges() {
		t.Errorf("nil ProfileDiff HasChanges")
	}
}

func TestProfileDiff(t *testing.T) {
	a := NewEvalProfile()
	a.increment("data.a", 2, 1)
	a.increment("data.b", 3, 2)
	a.increment("data.c", 5, 5)

	b := NewEvalProfile()
	b.increment("data.b", 3, 2)
	b.increment("data.c", 6, 5)
	b.increment("data.d", 1, 1)

	diff := a.Diff(b)
	if diff == nil || !diff.HasChanges() {
		t.Fatalf("expected changes, got %v", diff)
	}
	if len(diff.Added) != 1 || diff.Added["data.d"] == nil {
		t.Errorf("added = %v", diff.Added)
	}
	if len(diff.Removed) != 1 || diff.Removed["data.a"] == nil {
		t.Errorf("removed = %v", diff.Removed)
	}
	if len(diff.Changed) != 1 || diff.Changed["data.c"] == nil {
		t.Errorf("changed = %v", diff.Changed)
	}
	if delta := diff.Changed["data.c"]; delta.EvalsDelta != 1 || delta.SuccessesDelta != 0 {
		t.Errorf("changed delta = %+v", delta)
	}

	noDiff := a.Diff(a)
	if noDiff == nil || noDiff.HasChanges() {
		t.Errorf("expected no changes")
	}
	if noDiff.Added != nil || noDiff.Removed != nil || noDiff.Changed != nil {
		t.Errorf("expected nil fields for no changes, got %+v", noDiff)
	}
}

func TestProfileMerge(t *testing.T) {
	var nilp *EvalProfile
	if nilp.Merge(nil) != nil {
		t.Errorf("both nil merge should be nil")
	}
	p := NewEvalProfile()
	p.increment("data.a", 1, 0)
	if nilp.Merge(p) != p || p.Merge(nil) != p {
		t.Errorf("merge with nil should return non-nil side")
	}
	q := NewEvalProfile()
	q.increment("data.a", 2, 2)
	q.increment("data.b", 1, 1)
	merged := p.Merge(q)
	if merged.Stat("data.a").Evals != 3 || merged.Stat("data.a").Successes != 2 {
		t.Errorf("merged a = %v", merged.Stat("data.a"))
	}
	if merged.Stat("data.b") == nil {
		t.Errorf("merged b missing")
	}
}

// Diff compares the receiver with other and returns the differences. Added
// contains rules only present in other, Removed contains rules only present in
// the receiver, and Changed contains shared rules whose counts differ (deltas
// are other minus receiver). All fields are nil when empty.
func (p *EvalProfile) Diff(other *EvalProfile) *ProfileDiff {
	if p == nil {
		return nil
	}
	diff := &ProfileDiff{}
	if other != nil {
		for path, stat := range other.rules {
			if _, ok := p.rules[path]; !ok {
				if diff.Added == nil {
					diff.Added = map[string]*RuleStat{}
				}
				diff.Added[path] = stat.copy()
			}
		}
	}
	for path, stat := range p.rules {
		otherStat, ok := other.rules[path]
		if !ok {
			if diff.Removed == nil {
				diff.Removed = map[string]*RuleStat{}
			}
			diff.Removed[path] = stat.copy()
		} else if *stat != *otherStat {
			if diff.Changed == nil {
				diff.Changed = map[string]*RuleStatDelta{}
			}
			diff.Changed[path] = &RuleStatDelta{
				EvalsDelta:     otherStat.Evals - stat.Evals,
				SuccessesDelta: otherStat.Successes - stat.Successes,
			}
		}
	}
	return diff
}

// Copyright 2024 The OPA Authors.  All rights reserved.
// Use of this source code is governed by an Apache2
// license that can be found in the LICENSE file.

//go:build profile

package rego

import (
	"encoding/json"
	"fmt"
	"sort"
	"strings"

	"github.com/open-policy-agent/opa/v1/ast"
	"github.com/open-policy-agent/opa/v1/topdown"
)

// RuleStat represents the aggregate evaluation statistics for a single rule
// path.
type RuleStat struct {
	Evals     int `json:"evals"`
	Successes int `json:"successes"`
}

// SuccessRate returns the ratio of successes to evaluations. It returns 0 if
// the receiver is nil or if there are no evaluations recorded.
func (s *RuleStat) SuccessRate() float64 {
	if s == nil || s.Evals == 0 {
		return 0
	}
	return float64(s.Successes) / float64(s.Evals)
}

// String returns a human readable representation of the rule statistics.
func (s *RuleStat) String() string {
	if s == nil {
		return "<nil>"
	}
	return fmt.Sprintf("evals=%d successes=%d", s.Evals, s.Successes)
}

// EvalProfile maps fully qualified rule paths to evaluation statistics.
type EvalProfile struct {
	rules map[string]*RuleStat
}

// NewEvalProfile returns a new empty evaluation profile.
func NewEvalProfile() *EvalProfile {
	return &EvalProfile{rules: map[string]*RuleStat{}}
}

// Stat returns the rule statistics for the given fully qualified rule path, or
// nil if the path is not tracked.
func (p *EvalProfile) Stat(rule string) *RuleStat {
	if p == nil {
		return nil
	}
	return p.rules[rule]
}

// RulePaths returns the sorted set of tracked rule paths. It returns nil if
// there are no tracked paths.
func (p *EvalProfile) RulePaths() []string {
	if p == nil || len(p.rules) == 0 {
		return nil
	}
	paths := make([]string, 0, len(p.rules))
	for path := range p.rules {
		paths = append(paths, path)
	}
	sort.Strings(paths)
	return paths
}

// SuccessRate returns the success rate for the given rule path. It returns 0
// if the path is not tracked or has no evaluations.
func (p *EvalProfile) SuccessRate(rule string) float64 {
	if p == nil {
		return 0
	}
	if stat := p.rules[rule]; stat != nil {
		return stat.SuccessRate()
	}
	return 0
}

// OverallSuccessRate returns the aggregate success rate across all tracked
// rules. It returns 0 if the receiver is nil or there are no evaluations.
func (p *EvalProfile) OverallSuccessRate() float64 {
	if p == nil {
		return 0
	}
	var evals, successes int
	for _, stat := range p.rules {
		evals += stat.Evals
		successes += stat.Successes
	}
	if evals == 0 {
		return 0
	}
	return float64(successes) / float64(evals)
}

// HotRules returns the sorted set of rule paths whose evaluation count is at
// least minEvals. It returns nil if no rules qualify.
func (p *EvalProfile) HotRules(minEvals int) []string {
	if p == nil {
		return nil
	}
	var paths []string
	for path, stat := range p.rules {
		if stat.Evals >= minEvals {
			paths = append(paths, path)
		}
	}
	if len(paths) == 0 {
		return nil
	}
	sort.Strings(paths)


STEP 111 CALL 1 RETURN 0
COMMAND:
cd /app && sed -n '120,460p' v1/rego/profile.go
OUTPUT:
	sort.Strings(paths)
	return paths
}

// FailedRules returns the sorted set of rule paths that were evaluated but
// never succeeded. It returns nil if no rules qualify.
func (p *EvalProfile) FailedRules() []string {
	if p == nil {
		return nil
	}
	var paths []string
	for path, stat := range p.rules {
		if stat.Evals > 0 && stat.Successes == 0 {
			paths = append(paths, path)
		}
	}
	if len(paths) == 0 {
		return nil
	}
	sort.Strings(paths)
	return paths
}

// SucceededRules returns the sorted set of rule paths that succeeded at least
// once. It returns nil if no rules qualify.
func (p *EvalProfile) SucceededRules() []string {
	if p == nil {
		return nil
	}
	var paths []string
	for path, stat := range p.rules {
		if stat.Successes > 0 {
			paths = append(paths, path)
		}
	}
	if len(paths) == 0 {
		return nil
	}
	sort.Strings(paths)
	return paths
}

// Packages returns the sorted set of unique package names derived from the
// tracked rule paths. For example, the rule path "data.authz.allow" yields the
// package "data.authz".
func (p *EvalProfile) Packages() []string {
	if p == nil {
		return nil
	}
	seen := map[string]struct{}{}
	for path := range p.rules {
		if pkg := packageFromRulePath(path); pkg != "" {
			seen[pkg] = struct{}{}
		}
	}
	if len(seen) == 0 {
		return nil
	}
	pkgs := make([]string, 0, len(seen))
	for pkg := range seen {
		pkgs = append(pkgs, pkg)
	}
	sort.Strings(pkgs)
	return pkgs
}

func packageFromRulePath(path string) string {
	idx := strings.LastIndex(path, ".")
	if idx < 0 {
		return ""
	}
	return path[:idx]
}

// FilterByPackage returns a new profile containing deep-copied statistics for
// the rules belonging to the given package. It returns nil if the receiver is
// nil.
func (p *EvalProfile) FilterByPackage(pkg string) *EvalProfile {
	if p == nil {
		return nil
	}
	filtered := NewEvalProfile()
	for path, stat := range p.rules {
		if packageFromRulePath(path) == pkg {
			filtered.rules[path] = stat.copy()
		}
	}
	return filtered
}

// Merge returns a new profile combining the statistics of the receiver and
// other. Counts for rules present in both profiles are summed. If both are nil
// nil is returned; if only one is nil the non-nil profile is returned.
func (p *EvalProfile) Merge(other *EvalProfile) *EvalProfile {
	if p == nil && other == nil {
		return nil
	}
	if p == nil {
		return other
	}
	if other == nil {
		return p
	}
	merged := NewEvalProfile()
	for path, stat := range p.rules {
		merged.rules[path] = stat.copy()
	}
	for path, stat := range other.rules {
		if existing, ok := merged.rules[path]; ok {
			existing.Evals += stat.Evals
			existing.Successes += stat.Successes
		} else {
			merged.rules[path] = stat.copy()
		}
	}
	return merged
}

// PackageStats returns the aggregate statistics per package derived from the
// tracked rule paths.
func (p *EvalProfile) PackageStats() map[string]*RuleStat {
	if p == nil {
		return nil
	}
	stats := map[string]*RuleStat{}
	for path, stat := range p.rules {
		pkg := packageFromRulePath(path)
		agg := stats[pkg]
		if agg == nil {
			agg = &RuleStat{}
			stats[pkg] = agg
		}
		agg.Evals += stat.Evals
		agg.Successes += stat.Successes
	}
	return stats
}

// ContainsRule reports whether the given fully qualified rule path is tracked.
func (p *EvalProfile) ContainsRule(path string) bool {
	if p == nil {
		return false
	}
	_, ok := p.rules[path]
	return ok
}

// Summary returns a one-line summary of the profile.
func (p *EvalProfile) Summary() string {
	if p == nil {
		return "profile: disabled"
	}
	var evals, successes int
	for _, stat := range p.rules {
		evals += stat.Evals
		successes += stat.Successes
	}
	return fmt.Sprintf("profile: %d rules, %d evals, %d successes", len(p.rules), evals, successes)
}

// Equal reports whether the receiver and other have structurally identical
// statistics. Two nil profiles are equal.
func (p *EvalProfile) Equal(other *EvalProfile) bool {
	if p == nil || other == nil {
		return p == nil && other == nil
	}
	if len(p.rules) != len(other.rules) {
		return false
	}
	for path, stat := range p.rules {
		otherStat, ok := other.rules[path]
		if !ok || *stat != *otherStat {
			return false
		}
	}
	return true
}

// String returns a multi-line representation of the profile.
func (p *EvalProfile) String() string {
	if p == nil {
		return "<nil>"
	}
	var buf strings.Builder
	buf.WriteString("Profile:\n")
	for _, path := range p.RulePaths() {
		stat := p.rules[path]
		fmt.Fprintf(&buf, "  %s: evals=%d successes=%d\n", path, stat.Evals, stat.Successes)
	}
	return buf.String()
}

// MarshalJSON serializes the profile as a map of rule paths to stats.
func (p *EvalProfile) MarshalJSON() ([]byte, error) {
	if p == nil {
		return []byte("null"), nil
	}
	return json.Marshal(p.rules)
}

// ProfileDiff describes the differences between two profiles.
type ProfileDiff struct {
	Added   map[string]*RuleStat      `json:"added,omitempty"`
	Removed map[string]*RuleStat      `json:"removed,omitempty"`
	Changed map[string]*RuleStatDelta `json:"changed,omitempty"`
}

// RuleStatDelta represents the difference in rule statistics between two
// profiles.
type RuleStatDelta struct {
	EvalsDelta     int `json:"evals_delta"`
	SuccessesDelta int `json:"successes_delta"`
}

// HasChanges reports whether any of the diff fields are populated.
func (d *ProfileDiff) HasChanges() bool {
	if d == nil {
		return false
	}
	return len(d.Added) > 0 || len(d.Removed) > 0 || len(d.Changed) > 0
}

// Diff compares the receiver with other and returns the differences. Added
// contains rules only present in other, Removed contains rules only present in
// the receiver, and Changed contains shared rules whose counts differ (deltas
// are other minus receiver). All fields are nil when empty.
func (p *EvalProfile) Diff(other *EvalProfile) *ProfileDiff {
	if p == nil {
		return nil
	}
	diff := &ProfileDiff{}
	if other != nil {
		for path, stat := range other.rules {
			if _, ok := p.rules[path]; !ok {
				if diff.Added == nil {
					diff.Added = map[string]*RuleStat{}
				}
				diff.Added[path] = stat.copy()
			}
		}
	}
	for path, stat := range p.rules {
		var otherStat *RuleStat
		ok := false
		if other != nil {
			otherStat, ok = other.rules[path]
		}
		if !ok {
			if diff.Removed == nil {
				diff.Removed = map[string]*RuleStat{}
			}
			diff.Removed[path] = stat.copy()
		} else if *stat != *otherStat {
			if diff.Changed == nil {
				diff.Changed = map[string]*RuleStatDelta{}
			}
			diff.Changed[path] = &RuleStatDelta{
				EvalsDelta:     otherStat.Evals - stat.Evals,
				SuccessesDelta: otherStat.Successes - stat.Successes,
			}
		}
	}
	return diff
}

func (s *RuleStat) copy() *RuleStat {
	if s == nil {
		return nil
	}
	return &RuleStat{Evals: s.Evals, Successes: s.Successes}
}

func (p *EvalProfile) increment(path string, evals, successes int) {
	if p == nil {
		return
	}
	if p.rules == nil {
		p.rules = map[string]*RuleStat{}
	}
	stat := p.rules[path]
	if stat == nil {
		stat = &RuleStat{}
		p.rules[path] = stat
	}
	stat.Evals += evals
	stat.Successes += successes
}

// ruleProfilerAdapter adapts an EvalProfile to the topdown.RuleProfiler
// interface.
//
// Successes are counted once per rule entry that produces at least one result
// (rather than once per result). The stack tracks rule entries so that
// recursive rules and rules producing multiple results are counted correctly.
type ruleProfilerAdapter struct {
	profile *EvalProfile
	stack   []*ast.Rule
}

func (a *ruleProfilerAdapter) EnterRule(rule *ast.Rule) {
	if a == nil || a.profile == nil || rule == nil {
		return
	}
	a.profile.increment(rule.Path().String(), 1, 0)
	a.stack = append(a.stack, rule)
}

func (a *ruleProfilerAdapter) SucceedRule(rule *ast.Rule) {
	if a == nil || a.profile == nil || rule == nil {
		return
	}
	if n := len(a.stack); n > 0 && a.stack[n-1] == rule {
		a.stack = a.stack[:n-1]
		a.profile.increment(rule.Path().String(), 0, 1)
	}
}

func (a *ruleProfilerAdapter) FailRule(rule *ast.Rule) {
	if a == nil || a.profile == nil || rule == nil {
		return
	}
	if n := len(a.stack); n > 0 && a.stack[n-1] == rule {
		a.stack = a.stack[:n-1]
	}
}

// setupRuleProfile wires rule evaluation profiling into the query when
// enabled by either the construction or per-eval option.
func setupRuleProfile(r *Rego, ectx *EvalContext, q *topdown.Query) *EvalProfile {
	enabled := ectx.enableRuleProfile
	if !ectx.ruleProfileSet {
		enabled = r.enableRuleProfile
	}
	if !enabled {
		return nil
	}
	profile := NewEvalProfile()
	ectx.ruleProfile = profile
	q.WithRuleProfiler(&ruleProfilerAdapter{profile: profile})
	return profile
}

//go:build profile

package rego

import (
	"context"
	"testing"
)

func TestScratchEdgePaths(t *testing.T) {
	ctx := context.Background()
	module := `package authz
allow { true }

package authz.sub
thing { true }

package authz.partial
p contains x if { x := input.items[_] }
`
	r := New(
		Module("test.rego", module),
		Input(map[string]any{"items": []any{1, 2}}),
		EnableRuleProfile(true),
		Query("data.authz; data.authz.sub.thing; data.authz.partial.p"),
	)
	rs, err := r.Eval(ctx)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("eval: %v", err)
	}
	if len(rs) == 0 {
		t.Fatal("no results")
	}
	p := rs[0].Profile
	t.Logf("summary: %s", p.Summary())
	for _, path := range p.RulePaths() {
		t.Logf("  %s: %s", path, p.Stat(path))
	}
	if !p.ContainsRule("data.authz.allow") {
		t.Errorf("missing data.authz.allow")
	}
	if !p.ContainsRule("data.authz.sub.thing") {
		t.Errorf("missing data.authz.sub.thing")
	}
	if !p.ContainsRule("data.authz.partial.p") {
		t.Errorf("missing data.authz.partial.p")
	}
}

//go:build profile

package rego

import (
	"context"

)

func TestScratchEdgePaths(t *testing.T) {
	ctx := context.Background()
	r := New(
		Module("a.rego", `package authz
allow if { true }
`),
		Module("b.rego", `package authz.sub
thing if { true }
`),
		Module("c.rego", `package authz.partial
p contains x if { x := input.items[_] }
`),
		Input(map[string]any{"items": []any{1, 2}}),
		EnableRuleProfile(true),
		Query("data.authz; data.authz.sub.thing; data.authz.partial.p"),
	)
	rs, err := r.Eval(ctx)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("eval: %v", err)
	}
	if len(rs) == 0 {
		t.Fatal("no results")
	}
	p := rs[0].Profile
	t.Logf("summary: %s", p.Summary())
	for _, path := range p.RulePaths() {
		t.Logf("  %s: %s", path, p.Stat(path))
	}
	if !p.ContainsRule("data.authz.allow") {
		t.Errorf("missing data.authz.allow")
	}
	if !p.ContainsRule("data.authz.sub.thing") {
		t.Errorf("missing data.authz.sub.thing")
	}
	if !p.ContainsRule("data.authz.partial.p") {
		t.Errorf("missing data.authz.partial.p")
	}
	if got := p.Stat("data.authz.partial.p").Successes; got != 1 {
		t.Errorf("partial p successes = %d, want 1 (per-entry)", got)
	}
}

//go:build profile

package rego

import (
	"context"

func TestProfileEvaluation(t *testing.T) {
	ctx := context.Background()
	module := `package authz

allow if {
	input.user == "admin"
}

deny if {
	false
}

multi if {
	false
}

multi if {
	true
}

roles contains x if {
	x := input.roles[_]
}
`
	r := New(
		Module("test.rego", module),
		Input(map[string]any{"user": "admin", "roles": []any{"a", "b", "c"}}),
		EnableRuleProfile(true),
		Query("data.authz"),
	)

	rs, err := r.Eval(ctx)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("eval: %v", err)
	}
	if len(rs) == 0 {
		t.Fatal("expected results")
	}

	profile := rs[0].Profile
	if profile == nil {
		t.Fatal("expected profile to be set")
	}

	expected := map[string]RuleStat{
		"data.authz.allow": {Evals: 1, Successes: 1},
...
// Copyright 2024 The OPA Authors.  All rights reserved.
// Use of this source code is governed by an Apache2
// license that can be found in the LICENSE file.

//go:build profile

package rego_test

import (
	"context"
	"testing"

	"github.com/open-policy-agent/opa/rego"
)

func TestProfilePublicAPI(t *testing.T) {
	ctx := context.Background()
	r := rego.New(
		rego.Module("test.rego", `package authz
allow { input.user == "admin" }`),
		rego.Input(map[string]any{"user": "admin"}),
		rego.EnableRuleProfile(true),
		rego.Query("data.authz.allow"),
	)

	rs, err := r.Eval(ctx)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("eval: %v", err)
	}
	if len(rs) == 0 {
		t.Fatal("expected results")
	}
	profile := rs[0].Profile
	if profile == nil {
		t.Fatal("expected profile")
	}
	if profile.Summary() != "profile: 1 rules, 1 evals, 1 successes" {
		t.Errorf("summary = %q", profile.Summary())
	}
	if !profile.ContainsRule("data.authz.allow") {
		t.Errorf("allow missing from profile")
	}
	if stat := profile.Stat("data.authz.allow"); stat == nil || stat.SuccessRate() != 1 {
		t.Errorf("unexpected stat: %v", stat)
	}

	pq, err := r.PrepareForEval(ctx)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("prepare: %v", err)
	}
	rs2, err := pq.Eval(ctx, rego.EvalRuleProfile(true))
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("prepared eval: %v", err)
	}
	if len(rs2) == 0 || rs2[0].Profile == nil {
		t.Fatalf("expected profile on prepared eval")
	}

	// Externally constructed profiles should support the documented methods.
	p := &rego.EvalProfile{Rules: map[string]*rego.RuleStat{
		"data.authz.allow": {Evals: 2, Successes: 1},
	}}
	if !p.ContainsRule("data.authz.allow") {
		t.Errorf("externally constructed profile missing rule")
	}
	if p.SuccessRate("data.authz.allow") != 0.5 {
		t.Errorf("success rate = %v", p.SuccessRate("data.authz.allow"))
	}
	if p.Summary() != "profile: 1 rules, 2 evals, 1 successes" {
		t.Errorf("summary = %q", p.Summary())
	}
}

COMMAND:
cd /app && git commit -m "rego: add opt-in rule evaluation profiling

Add an EvalProfile that records per-rule evaluation and success counts
during Rego evaluation. The feature is gated behind the 'profile' build
tag.

- EvalProfile maps fully qualified rule paths to *RuleStat with Evals
  and Successes counts. Every entered rule appears, including failed
  rules; rules with multiple definitions are entered once per definition.
- Result gains a Profile field that is populated when profiling is
  enabled via EnableRuleProfile (construction) or EvalRuleProfile
  (per-eval).
- Add RuleStat, ProfileDiff, and RuleStatDelta types with the documented
  methods (sorting, filtering, merging, diffing, summaries, and string
  representations).
- Wire topdown evaluation to a RuleProfiler interface that feeds rule
  entry/success/failure events back to the rego package.

Signed-off-by: OPA Automation" && git log --oneline -3
